cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(black_scholes_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(black_scholes black_scholes.cpp)

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT_2
#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT1_2
#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI
#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948
#endif

enum OptionType {
    CALL = 0,
    PUT = 1
};

struct OptionInput {
    int type;           // CALL or PUT
    double strike;      // Strike price
    double spot;        // Spot price
    double q;           // Dividend yield
    double r;           // Risk-free rate
    double t;           // Time to maturity
    double vol;         // Volatility
    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)
    double tol;         // Tolerance
};

// Standard normal cumulative distribution function
inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
}

// Standard normal probability density function
inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {
    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);
}

// Black-Scholes formula for European options
double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
    const double S = option.spot;
    const double K = option.strike;
    const double r = option.r;
    const double q = option.q;
    const double T = option.t;
    const double sigma = option.vol;
    
    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));
    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
    
    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
    
    double price;
    if (option.type == CALL) {
        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    } else { // PUT
        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
    }
    
    return price;
}

// Standard test cases for validation
inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
    return {{
        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
    }};
}

// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {
    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
    options.resize(numOptions);
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
        options[i] = base;
        
        // Add some variation for larger datasets
        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
        options[i].spot *= factor;
        options[i].strike *= factor;
    }
}

bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
    bool allPassed = true;
    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
    
    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
        const double computed = results[i];
        const double expected = options[i].value;
        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
        
        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
               i, computed, expected, relError);
        
        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
            allPassed = false;
        }
    }
    
    return allPassed;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numOptions = 10000;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate options
    std::vector<OptionInput> options;
    generateOptions(options, numOptions);
    
    // Allocate results
    std::vector<double> results(numOptions);
    
    // Price options
    printf("Pricing options...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        results[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration.count() / 1000.0);
    printf("Options per second: %.0f\n", numOptions / (duration.count() / 1e6));
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating results...\n");
        bool valid = validateResults(options, results);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <array>

#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT_2
#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT1_2
#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI
#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948
#endif

enum OptionType {
    CALL = 0,
    PUT = 1
};

struct OptionInput {
    int type;           // CALL or PUT
    double strike;      // Strike price
    double spot;        // Spot price
    double q;           // Dividend yield
    double r;           // Risk-free rate
    double t;           // Time to maturity
    double vol;         // Volatility
    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)
    double tol;         // Tolerance
};

// Standard normal cumulative distribution function
inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
}

// Standard normal probability density function
inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {
    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);
}

// Black-Scholes formula for European options
double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
    const double S = option.spot;
    const double K = option.strike;
    const double r = option.r;
    const double q = option.q;
    const double T = option.t;
    const double sigma = option.vol;
    
    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));
    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
    
    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
    
    double price;
    if (option.type == CALL) {
        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    } else { // PUT
        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
    }
    
    return price;
}

        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
    }};
}

// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {
    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
    options.resize(numOptions);
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
        options[i] = base;
        
        // Add some variation for larger datasets
        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
        options[i].spot *= factor;
        options[i].strike *= factor;
    }
}

bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
    bool allPassed = true;
    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
    
    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
        const double computed = results[i];
        const double expected = options[i].value;
        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
        
        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
               i, computed, expected, relError);
        
        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
            allPassed = false;
        }
    }
    
    return allPassed;
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numOptions = 10000;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate options
    std::vector<OptionInput> options;
    generateOptions(options, numOptions);
    
    // Allocate results
    std::vector<double> results(numOptions);
    
    // Price options
    printf("Pricing options...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        results[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration.count() / 1000.0);
    printf("Options per second: %.0f\n", numOptions / (duration.count() / 1e6));
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating results...\n");
        bool valid = validateResults(options, results);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+struct OptionRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <climits>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <exception>
#include <vector>

#include <mpi.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT_2
#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT1_2
#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI
#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948
#endif

enum OptionType {
    CALL = 0,
    PUT = 1
};

struct OptionInput {
    int type;           // CALL or PUT
    double strike;      // Strike price
    double spot;        // Spot price
    double q;           // Dividend yield
    double r;           // Risk-free rate
    double t;           // Time to maturity
    double vol;         // Volatility
    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)
    double tol;         // Tolerance
};

// Standard normal cumulative distribution function
inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
}

// Standard normal probability density function
inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {
    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);
}

// Black-Scholes formula for European options
double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
    const double S = option.spot;
    const double K = option.strike;
    const double r = option.r;
    const double q = option.q;
    const double T = option.t;
    const double sigma = option.vol;
    
    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));
    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
    
    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
    
    double price;
    if (option.type == CALL) {
        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    } else { // PUT
        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
    }
    
    return price;
}

// Standard test cases for validation
inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
    return {{
        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
    }};
}

// Generate from the global index so every partition reproduces the serial input.
inline OptionInput generateOption(const size_t index) noexcept {
    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
    OptionInput option = testOptions[index % testOptions.size()];
    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (index / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
    option.spot *= factor;
    option.strike *= factor;
    return option;
}

bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
    bool allPassed = true;
    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
    
    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
        const double computed = results[i];
        const double expected = options[i].value;
        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
        
        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
               i, computed, expected, relError);
        
        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
            allPassed = false;
        }
    }
    
    return allPassed;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

struct OptionRange {
    size_t first;
    size_t count;
};

OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
}

// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
                                  const OptionRange localRange,
                                  const size_t numOptions,
                                  const size_t outputCount,
                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
    std::vector<double> results;
    std::vector<int> counts;
    std::vector<int> displacements;
    if (rank == 0) {
        results.resize(outputCount);
        counts.resize(numRanks);
        displacements.resize(numRanks);
    }

    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
        const size_t last = first + count;
        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;

        if (rank == 0) {
            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
            }
        }
        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        first = last;
    }
    return results;
}

int runBenchmark(int argc, char** argv, const int rank, const int numRanks) {
    unsigned long long optionCount = 10000;
    // Validation, result output, and command-line status (run/help/error).
    int settings[3] = {0, 0, 0};
    if (rank == 0) {
        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
                optionCount = static_cast<size_t>(atoll(argv[++i]));
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
                settings[0] = 1;
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
                settings[1] = 1;
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
                printUsage(argv[0]);
                settings[2] = 1;
                break;
            } else {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
                settings[2] = 2;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    MPI_Bcast(settings, 3, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    if (settings[2] != 0) {
        return settings[2] - 1;
    }
    MPI_Bcast(&optionCount, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const size_t numOptions = static_cast<size_t>(optionCount);
    const bool validate = settings[0] != 0;
    const bool printResults = settings[1] != 0;

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
        printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
        printf("Pricing options...\n");
    }

    const OptionRange localRange = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
    std::vector<double> localResults(localRange.count);

    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
    // Fuse local generation and pricing to avoid storing or communicating inputs.
    for (size_t i = 0; i < localRange.count; ++i) {
        localResults[i] = blackScholes(generateOption(localRange.first + i));
    }
    const double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double duration = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration * 1000.0);
        printf("Options per second: %.0f\n",
               duration > 0.0 ? numOptions / duration : 0.0);
    }

    int status = 0;
    if (printResults || validate) {
        const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), numOptions);
        const size_t outputCount = printResults ? numOptions : numChecks;
        const std::vector<double> results = gatherResults(
            localResults, localRange, numOptions, outputCount, rank, numRanks);
        if (rank == 0) {
            if (printResults) {
                print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
            }
            if (validate) {
                std::vector<OptionInput> options(numChecks);
                for (size_t i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
                    options[i] = generateOption(i);
                }
                printf("Validating results...\n");
                const bool valid = validateResults(options, results);
                printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
                status = valid ? 0 : 1;
            }
        }
        if (validate) {
            MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        }
    }
    return status;
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    if (MPI_Init(&argc, &argv) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
        return 1;
    }
    int rank = 0;
    int numRanks = 1;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);

    int status = 0;
    try {
        status = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank, numRanks);
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
    MPI_Finalize();
    return status;
}

# MPI is required even for single-process runs.
target_link_libraries(black_scholes PRIVATE MPI::MPI_CXX m)

-            printUsage(argv[0]);

+struct OptionRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+
+int runBenchmark(int argc, char** argv, const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    unsigned long long optionCount = 10000;

+                optionCount = static_cast<size_t>(atoll(argv[++i]));
+            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
+                settings[0] = 1;
+            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
+                settings[1] = 1;
+            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
+                printUsage(argv[0]);
+                settings[2] = 1;
+                break;
+            } else {
+                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
+                printUsage(argv[0]);
+                settings[2] = 2;
+                break;
+            }
         }

+        localResults[i] = blackScholes(generateOption(localRange.first + i));
+    }
+    const double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double duration = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+                status = valid ? 0 : 1;

+        if (validate) {
+            MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }

import subprocess
import re
serial = '/tmp/black_scholes_serial_mpi_r5'
parallel = '/tmp/black_scholes_mpi_r5_build/black_scholes'
def stable(text):
    return '\\n'.join(line for line in text.splitlines() if not line.startswith(('Computation time:', 'Options per second:')))
for n in [0, 1, 3, 7, 8, 10, 17, 31, 10001, 100003]:
    args = ['-n', str(n), '-r', '-v']
    reference = subprocess.run([serial, *args], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
    for ranks in [1, 2, 4, 8]:
        result = subprocess.run(['mpirun', '--oversubscribe', '--bind-to', 'core', '-np', str(ranks), parallel, *args], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
        assert result.returncode == 0, (n, ranks, result.stderr)
        if stable(result.stdout) "'!= stable(reference.stdout):
            print(f'"'MISMATCH n={n}, ranks={ranks}')
            print('SERIAL:', reference.stdout)
            print('MPI:', result.stdout)
            raise SystemExit(1)
    print(f'PASS n={n}: exact serial output with 1, 2, 4, and 8 ranks', flush=True)

+struct OptionRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

+struct OptionRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+

+

+    const double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double duration = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration * 1000.0);
+        printf("Options per second: %.0f\n",
+               duration > 0.0 ? numOptions / duration : 0.0);
+    }
+
+    int status = 0;
+    if (printResults || validate) {
+        const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), numOptions);
+        const size_t outputCount = printResults ? numOptions : numChecks;
+        const std::vector<double> results = gatherResults(
+            localResults, localRange, numOptions, outputCount, rank, numRanks);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            if (printResults) {
+                print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
+            }
+            if (validate) {
+                std::vector<OptionInput> options(numChecks);
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
+                    options[i] = generateOption(i);
+                }
+                printf("Validating results...\n");
+                const bool valid = validateResults(options, results);
+                printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+                status = valid ? 0 : 1;
+            }
         }
+        if (validate) {
+            MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+    return status;

+struct OptionRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+

+struct OptionRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

+int runBenchmark(int argc, char** argv, const int rank, const int numRanks) {

+struct OptionRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+

+struct OptionRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};

+OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;

+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return status;

+struct OptionRange {
+    size_t first;

+OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);

+struct OptionRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;

+OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

serial = '/tmp/black_scholes_serial_mpi_r5'
parallel = '/tmp/black_scholes_mpi_r5_build/black_scholes'

subprocess.run(compile_cmd, input=source, text=True, check=True)
def stable(text):
    return '\\n'.join(line for line in text.splitlines() if not line.startswith(('Computation time:', 'Options per second:')))
cases = [(parallel, 8, ['-n', '0', '-r', '-v']), (parallel, 8, ['-n', '1', '-r', '-v']), (parallel, 3, ['-n', '17', '-r', '-v']), (parallel, 4, ['-n', '31', '-v']), (parallel, 4, ['-n', '100003', '-r']), (parallel, 8, ['-n', '1000003', '-r', '-v']), (parallel, 3, ['-v']), (parallel, 3, ['-n', '257']), (chunked, 3, ['-n', '97', '-r', '-v']), (chunked, 8, ['-n', '21', '-r', '-v'])]
for program, ranks, args in cases:
    reference = subprocess.run([serial, *args], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
    result = subprocess.run(['mpirun', '--oversubscribe', '--bind-to', 'core', '-np', str(ranks), program, *args], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    assert result.returncode == reference.returncode, (args, ranks, result.stderr)
    assert stable(result.stdout) == stable(reference.stdout), '\\n'.join(difflib.unified_diff(stable(reference.stdout).splitlines(), stable(result.stdout).splitlines()))
    print(f'PASS {\"chunked\" if program == chunked else \"MPI\"} ranks={ranks} args={args}', flush=True)

    result = subprocess.run(['mpirun', '-np', '3', parallel, *args], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    assert result.returncode == code, (args, result.returncode, result.stderr)
    expected = reference.stdout.replace(serial, parallel)
    assert result.stdout == expected, (args, expected, result.stdout)
    print(f'PASS collective command-line exit: {args}', flush=True)

+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

+    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);

+    try {
+        status = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank, numRanks);
+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return status;

    25	#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948

    27	
    28	enum OptionType {
    29	    CALL = 0,
    30	    PUT = 1
    31	};
    32	
    33	struct OptionInput {
    34	    int type;           // CALL or PUT
    35	    double strike;      // Strike price
    36	    double spot;        // Spot price
    37	    double q;           // Dividend yield
    38	    double r;           // Risk-free rate
    39	    double t;           // Time to maturity
    40	    double vol;         // Volatility
    41	    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)
    42	    double tol;         // Tolerance
    43	};
    44	
    45	struct PricingTerms {
    46	    double standardDeviation;
    47	    double discount;
    48	    double dividendDiscount;
    49	};
    50	
    51	// Standard normal cumulative distribution function
    52	inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    53	    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
    54	}
    55	
    56	// Standard normal probability density function
    57	inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {
    58	    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);
    59	}
    60	
    61	// Black-Scholes formula for European options
    62	inline double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option, const PricingTerms& terms) noexcept {
    63	    const double S = option.spot;
    64	    const double K = option.strike;
    65	    const double r = option.r;
    66	    const double q = option.q;
    67	    const double T = option.t;
    68	    const double sigma = option.vol;
    69	    
    70	    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
    71	        return 0.0;
    72	    }
    73	    
    74	    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / terms.standardDeviation;
    75	    const double d2 = d1 - terms.standardDeviation;
    76	    
    77	    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
    78	    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
    79	    const double discount = terms.discount;
    80	    
    81	    double price;
    82	    if (option.type == CALL) {
    83	        price = S * terms.dividendDiscount * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    84	    } else { // PUT
    85	        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * terms.dividendDiscount * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
    86	    }
    87	    
    88	    return price;
    89	}
    90	
    91	// Standard test cases for validation
    92	inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
    93	    return {{
    94	        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
    95	        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
    96	        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
    97	        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
    98	        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
    99	        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
   100	        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
   101	    }};
   102	}
   103	
   104	// Generate from the global index so every partition reproduces the serial input.
   105	inline OptionInput generateOption(const size_t index) noexcept {
   106	    static constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
   107	    OptionInput option = testOptions[index % testOptions.size()];
   108	    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (index / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
   109	    option.spot *= factor;
   110	    option.strike *= factor;
   111	    return option;
   112	}

   121	        const double computed = results[i];
   122	        const double expected = options[i].value;
   123	        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
   124	        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
   125	        
   155	    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
   156	    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
   157	    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
   158	    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
   159	    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
   160	            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
   161	}
   162	
   163	// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
   164	// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
   165	std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
   166	                                  const OptionRange localRange,
   167	                                  const size_t numOptions,
   168	                                  const size_t outputCount,
   169	                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
   170	    std::vector<double> results;
   171	    std::vector<int> counts;
   172	    std::vector<int> displacements;
   173	    if (rank == 0) {
   174	        results.resize(outputCount);
   175	        counts.resize(numRanks);
   176	        displacements.resize(numRanks);
   177	    }
   178	
   179	    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
   180	        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
   181	                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
   182	        const size_t last = first + count;
   183	        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
   184	        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
   185	        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
   186	            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
   187	        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
   188	            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
   189	
   190	        if (rank == 0) {
   191	            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
   192	                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
   193	                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
   194	                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
   195	                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
   196	                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
   197	                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
   198	                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
   199	            }
   200	        }
   201	        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
   202	                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
   203	                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
   204	                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   205	        first = last;
   206	    }
   207	    return results;
   208	}
   209

   233	    }

   247	        printf("Pricing options...\n");

   321	    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
   322	        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());

+struct OptionRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+

+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+

+                settings[1] = 1;
+            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
+                printUsage(argv[0]);
+                settings[2] = 1;
+                break;
+            } else {
+                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
+                printUsage(argv[0]);
+                settings[2] = 2;
+                break;
+            }
         }

codex
Implemented unconditional MPI parallelism with balanced partitions, local input generation, cached pricing terms, and ordered gathers only when needed. CMake requires MPI; the executable remains `black_scholes`. Only the two existing benchmark files changed.

Verified exact serial results and hashes, empty and uneven partitions, chunked gathers, and error handling. A local 16-rank run priced 8 million options in 48 ms.

```bash
mpirun -np 8 ./black_scholes -n 1000000 -v -r
```

+struct OptionRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+OptionRange partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = numOptions / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % ranks;
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + static_cast<size_t>(index < remainder)};
+}
+
+// Gather only the requested prefix, preserving serial order. Windowing keeps
+// MPI counts and displacements valid even when the total exceeds INT_MAX.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const OptionRange localRange,
+                                  const size_t numOptions,
+                                  const size_t outputCount,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(outputCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < outputCount;) {
+        const size_t count = std::min(outputCount - first,
+                                      static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX));
+        const size_t last = first + count;
+        const size_t localFirst = std::max(first, localRange.first);
+        const size_t localLast = std::min(last, localRange.first + localRange.count);
+        const int sendCount = localLast > localFirst
+            ? static_cast<int>(localLast - localFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+            ? localResults.data() + (localFirst - localRange.first) : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+                const OptionRange range = partitionOptions(numOptions, source, numRanks);
+                const size_t sourceFirst = std::max(first, range.first);
+                const size_t sourceLast = std::min(last, range.first + range.count);
+                counts[source] = sourceLast > sourceFirst
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceLast - sourceFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[source] = counts[source] > 0
+                    ? static_cast<int>(sourceFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = last;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+

+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());

Implemented unconditional MPI parallelism with balanced partitions, local input generation, cached pricing terms, and ordered gathers only when needed. CMake requires MPI; the executable remains `black_scholes`. Only the two existing benchmark files changed.

Verified exact serial results and hashes, empty and uneven partitions, chunked gathers, and error handling. A local 16-rank run priced 8 million options in 48 ms.

```bash
mpirun -np 8 ./black_scholes -n 1000000 -v -r
```
